# Project plan

Last updated: 2026-10-26

The target remains the full, source-verified FFIX guide in the project brief. A working interface, a heading inventory, or a few authored chapters do not count as complete coverage.

See [TASK_BACKLOG.md](TASK_BACKLOG.md) for the ordered, evidence-based work queue, [CONTENT_COVERAGE.md](CONTENT_COVERAGE.md) for subsystem completion evidence, and [USER_SETUP_CHECKLIST.md](USER_SETUP_CHECKLIST.md) for consolidated account-owner actions.

## Current milestone: Phase 3 — completion systems

Latest milestone (2026-10-26): B02/B05 added Bohden Arch's Disc I Ether and Moccha Coffee as separate item paths and checklist objectives; the coffee is now represented as a key-item record. GameFAQs supplies the exact bridge/post and tree landmarks; Jegged independently confirms both pickups at South Gate/Bohden Arch, and RPGClassics cross-checks the coffee location and quest role. A regression test and data-audit invariant enforce the source, path, and objective links. `npm test` passes 45/45; lint, typecheck, data audit, production build, and `git diff --check` pass with 0 audit errors and 188 partial-coverage warnings across 44 chapters, 142 missables, 838 objectives, and 435 sources; all missables link to 100 cutoff windows. Catalog: 236 items and 1,133 paths across all 44 chapter slices. Build: 2,424.23 kB JavaScript (590.15 kB gzip), 61.11 kB CSS (12.91 kB gzip); Vite's chunk-size advisory remains. In-game and release behavior remain unverified; no owner-side setup action changed.
Previous milestone (2026-10-26): Added source-backed Valia Pira preparation guidance to the Desert Palace bestiary and pre-boss checkpoint. Level 36 is the boss's stat and no party-level target is set. The route emphasizes all six bloodstones, Body Temp, Reflect/Carbuncle, and optional MP depletion; Wind is supported by the stat tables while Water weakness remains disputed. The PlayStation Boss Guide's Medium rating is subjective and single-source. ### Next prioritized chunk

The next priority remains B02/B05: continue source-by-source review of item acquisition and shop windows, connect limited purchase paths to walkthrough checkpoints, and keep exact-copy loss separate from item-type recovery. B01 remains active for optional route branches, party splits, one-way cutoffs, and progression prerequisites. The gameplay route graph covers all 44 chapters with 96 links: 41 required story, two optional detours, 30 optional returns, and 23 rejoins; all 32 optional branches pass structural connectivity, while 100 cutoff windows cover all 142 missable warnings. The 111-edge objective graph contains 32 ATE-order, nine objective-access, and 70 quest-prerequisite links. The Hilda Garde returns and Disc IV Mognet itinerary are represented, including the Disc III Atla Mogshop stop; detailed trigger timing, remaining optional route research, and release verification remain open.

## Current blockers and limits

- The local repository has history on `main`, including baseline commit `0cec689`; no remote or live deployment is configured. GitHub API lookup and a direct push attempt to `Appl3Tree/FFIX-Guide` returned 404, which does not distinguish a missing repository from an access issue. The owner-side repository creation/access task and follow-up are documented in `USER_SETUP_CHECKLIST.md`; no push or deployment is claimed.
- The Playwright CLI wrapper could not retrieve its package metadata because registry DNS resolution failed. A read-only in-app browser snapshot confirmed the then-current 66-link Route Map and 41/2/12/11 counts at the default desktop viewport; this predates the current Memoria route slice. Custom responsive-width and accessibility checks remain open, and earlier responsive checks apply to previous route slices only.
- The walkthrough has 44 partially authored chapters out of 44; the standard route has been statically simulated through the ending, and the Excalibur II route has a source-based trace through the 11:13:07 pickup with profile timer/checkpoint support, but no independent timer run. Firebase sync, complete reference databases, remaining missables, alternative route budgets, and full release verification remain unfinished.
